##### A week filled with Christmas/Holiday campaigns, publishers trying to regain trust, fashion + beauty industry backlash, brand exits + revivals, and the usual chaos agency-brand business.

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of their brand work. 

> Brand & Advertising coverage from 25.10 — 07.11.2025, with new section covering ongoing sports season.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!JnHP!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/ab33dea1-17c7-4435-93f3-2a9b4932c308?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Was [Timothée Chalamet’s Vogue cover](https://substack.com/redirect/2e7de686-3dca-43a7-a521-f375b29b9bff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) really [so out](https://substack.com/redirect/b94d4f7b-f02b-4994-ae36-e48dee9ecdc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of this world?
* France moves to [suspend Shein](https://substack.com/redirect/88708821-6b67-405f-9bd0-5b1fdda63bf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over sex dolls as debut store opens in Paris.
* Tylenol, Kleenex, Band-Aid and [more put under one roof](https://substack.com/redirect/499d419a-0453-4430-a2ba-77dd63986b7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in $48.7 billion consumer brands deal.
* Starbucks sells [majority stake](https://substack.com/redirect/d761967c-a13c-4faf-b31f-c5615c9ec005?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in China business to Boyu Capital.
* Apple TV’s [colorful new intro](https://substack.com/redirect/a01bd02d-0545-4cf4-b4e2-311eeb6d485c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) was created with real glass and practical effects.

---

In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

##### Make big bets, [defend](https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with data.

Put your hand up if you’ve ever had to fight tooth and nail to get a slightly unhinged campaign signed off 🙋

And then defend it because, yes, the polar bear breaks up with their human girlfriend because she’s using an aerosol deodorant, not the natural, eco-friendly stuff, but it’s growing brand awareness, okay?!? (this is a [legitimate campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/4087e28c-c0e7-4b9b-a854-c03e49b6d9cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), thanks [Wild](https://substack.com/redirect/df9b666c-9a3a-4bb9-b7a7-39e92349e914?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)).

Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow and [prove](https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ROI of brand spend.

If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.

[Tracksuit’s](https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll get the budget for the next moderately chaotic idea.

* St Pancras has revealed its [stunning 2025 Christmas tree](https://substack.com/redirect/301fcb1b-3c9a-4b0c-b889-d888801b75a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) – and it’s a massive music box.
* Brands set to [cut open web display spend 30%](https://substack.com/redirect/daf2dca4-66ca-4bc0-b33e-bd26735a3c73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in response to AI search.

* Gen Z women are [outspending](https://substack.com/redirect/8f4228c7-538b-4827-8069-7d033d8d2206?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) male art collectors.
* The [end of Teen Vogue](https://substack.com/redirect/7de1181e-0e9c-4680-83b4-ce03036a21d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) completes fashion media’s retreat from politics.
* Media brands are trying hardd to [win over public trust](https://substack.com/redirect/072757d8-6ceb-4803-9a40-5796dff35717?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and grow their media business.

	+ “Biased,” “boring,” “chaotic,” and “bad”: A majority of teens hold [negative views of news media](https://substack.com/redirect/0b328eea-e6f1-490d-b282-e6dced9acebb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), report finds.

* Have you got a CEO or CFO who just doesn’t seem to get brand? [This guide](https://substack.com/redirect/c06752e5-cf23-4fcc-8982-b057be57b926?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will help you speak their language and secure the spend.\*\*
* [ESPN Bet to shut down](https://substack.com/redirect/9b11db4d-6873-4b1c-9d22-56f0ac8925ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as network signs new partnership with DraftKings amid NBA betting scandal.
* J. Press’s [preppy](https://substack.com/redirect/e81b13e1-6b3a-4ded-a300-2b75ab36d2d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) handbook.

> 🦀 Report Break: Deloitte study found [monthly average digital entertainment spending](https://substack.com/redirect/77e7524f-e7f6-4aa9-9bf6-98fe619fdfdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) rose 24% to $78 from $63 between 2024–2025, as Australians added more subscriptions and platforms successfully pushed through price increases.

> Households now hold an average of 3.7 subscriptions, up from 3.3 last year and 2.3 in 2021.

<h1 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;color: rgb(54,55,55);font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 900;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 0;line-height: 36px;font-size: 32px;"><a href="https://substack.com/app-link/post?publication\_id=712314&amp;post\_id=178268163&amp;utm\_source=post-email-title&amp;utm\_campaign=email-post-title&amp;isFreemail=true&amp;r=6q6egl&amp;token=eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjo0MDY3NjU3NDksInBvc3RfaWQiOjE3ODI2ODE2MywiaWF0IjoxNzYyNTU1MTU5LCJleHAiOjE3NjUxNDcxNTksImlzcyI6InB1Yi03MTIzMTQiLCJzdWIiOiJwb3N0LXJlYWN0aW9uIn0.ztT8S8vhX0kmfAUBqnYhxBM3k1lY4h0gAYAW1jGnANI" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: none;">Christmas marketing is Wicked, Media brands fight for trust, and the rest is marketing on steroids.</a></h1>
<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">A week filled with Christmas/Holiday campaigns, publishers trying to regain trust, fashion + beauty industry backlash, brand exits + revivals, and the usual chaos agency-brand business.</h3>

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of their brand work. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Brand &amp; Advertising coverage from 25.10 — 07.11.2025, with new section covering ongoing sports season.</p></blockquote>

</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Was </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e7de686-3dca-43a7-a521-f375b29b9bff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Timothée Chalamet’s Vogue cover</a><span> really </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b94d4f7b-f02b-4994-ae36-e48dee9ecdc2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">so out</a><span> of this world?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>France moves to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/88708821-6b67-405f-9bd0-5b1fdda63bf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">suspend Shein</a><span> over sex dolls as debut store opens in Paris.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Tylenol, Kleenex, Band-Aid and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/499d419a-0453-4430-a2ba-77dd63986b7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more put under one roof </a><span>in $48.7 billion consumer brands deal.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Starbucks sells </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d761967c-a13c-4faf-b31f-c5615c9ec005?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">majority stake</a><span> in China business to Boyu Capital.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple TV’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a01bd02d-0545-4cf4-b4e2-311eeb6d485c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">colorful new intro </a><span>was created with real glass and practical effects.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Make big bets, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">defend</a><span> with data.</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Put your hand up if you’ve ever had to fight tooth and nail to get a slightly unhinged campaign signed off 🙋</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And then defend it because, yes, the polar bear breaks up with their human girlfriend because she’s using an aerosol deodorant, not the natural, eco-friendly stuff, but it’s growing brand awareness, okay?!? (this is a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4087e28c-c0e7-4b9b-a854-c03e49b6d9cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">legitimate campaign</a><span>, thanks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/df9b666c-9a3a-4bb9-b7a7-39e92349e914?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wild</a><span>).</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="568"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b45d3290-5f08-4275-953b-87e93a6da7b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/bbfa18dd-5fbd-4f48-8d76-dae73d239f57\_1600x896.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":815,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":568,"bytes":null,"alt":"","title":"","type":null,"href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":null,"isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="307.864010989011" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!D3Jx!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbbfa18dd-5fbd-4f48-8d76-dae73d239f57\_1600x896.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" title="" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prove</a><span> the ROI of brand spend.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25ed2bb8-e5c0-4595-93fb-71ff95d112a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s </a><span>always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll get the budget for the next moderately chaotic idea.</span></p>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://www.gotracksuit.com?utm\_campaign=Creator%20Partnerships&amp;utm\_source=email&amp;utm\_medium=sponsored\_placement&amp;utm\_term=homepage&amp;utm\_content=The%20Social%20Juice","text":"Check out Tracksuit today!","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1a71f1a-543c-4912-9e4e-35b5b3d8659e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">Check out Tracksuit today!</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>St Pancras has revealed its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/301fcb1b-3c9a-4b0c-b889-d888801b75a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">stunning 2025 Christmas tree</a><span> – and it’s a massive music box.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands set to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/daf2dca4-66ca-4bc0-b33e-bd26735a3c73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cut open web display spend 30%</a><span> in response to AI search.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘Real magic’? Coca-Cola’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fdf36e99-4d62-447a-8020-dca87ebc87ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated Christmas ad</a><span> sparks widespread backlash (again).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z women are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8f4228c7-538b-4827-8069-7d033d8d2206?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">outspending</a><span> male art collectors.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7de1181e-0e9c-4680-83b4-ce03036a21d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">end of Teen Vogue</a><span> completes fashion media’s retreat from politics.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Media brands are trying hardd to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/072757d8-6ceb-4803-9a40-5796dff35717?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">win over public trust</a><span> and grow their media business.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>NBC News launches campaign to focus on ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/06af0a2d-a8f6-4986-9565-d42c62f91854?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reporting for America.</a><span>’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“Biased,” “boring,” “chaotic,” and “bad”: A majority of teens hold </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0b328eea-e6f1-490d-b282-e6dced9acebb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">negative views of news media</a><span>, report finds.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Puma cuts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad38dc24-f9d5-47d9-9682-dc1d2e37405a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">another 900 jobs</a><span> and asks for patience.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Have you got a CEO or CFO who just doesn’t seem to get brand? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c06752e5-cf23-4fcc-8982-b057be57b926?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">This guide</a><span> will help you speak their language and secure the spend.\*\*</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9b11db4d-6873-4b1c-9d22-56f0ac8925ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ESPN Bet to shut down</a><span> as network signs new partnership with DraftKings amid NBA betting scandal.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>J. Press’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e81b13e1-6b3a-4ded-a300-2b75ab36d2d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">preppy</a><span> handbook.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon is right to bring back ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4ab6f707-bf75-40fe-95ce-06bdcc02e2bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Joy Ride</a><span>,’ its most successful Christmas campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/82db92a0-8922-4d72-be68-39bcb92196c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Best Buy’s holiday marketing campaign</a><span> focuses on low-prices and nostalgia this season.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>This Christmas, IKEA reminds us that the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7733663f-307d-47b5-8c6a-39ddaf054e46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">best gift is being present</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/669a83d0-1131-426c-8c53-fe191877608a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Saint Laurent</a><span> dethrones Miu Miu as fashion’s hottest brand in Q3 2025.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Hunter Schafer, Nadia Lee Cohen partner for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c274204b-07a1-4d28-9198-a064579d1e6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gentle Monster short film</a><span> ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/953a2f35-c7ab-4137-8378-af059af6d374?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">THE HUNT</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Shay Mitchell’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/51d8e436-2dcc-49f3-a3a6-fd7997200979?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new children’s skin care brand rini</a><span> faces backlash on social media.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Olivier Rousteing departs </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/af59d224-7f45-43ab-9bc2-51f174e44a3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Balmain</a><span> after 14 years as creative director.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>L.L.Bean brings </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/01fecf8e-1b98-4273-bb88-bcc1ae8cfc96?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">holiday nostalgia home</a><span> in “Invited to the Holidays Since 1912.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gucci Altitude: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8ca6781-e54c-46c4-b87c-d26805dc5789?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jannik Sinner</a><span> stars in Gucci’s new winter sportswear collection.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Louis Vuitton </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5976d9bd-a53c-4011-b8f5-cc405271d91c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unveils</a><span> its 2025 holiday campaign “Le Voyage des Lumières.”</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dabdb111-313c-4554-b301-89e880c2923e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jude Bellingham</a><span>, Callum Turner and four-legged friends star in Louis Vuitton’s latest campaign.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Jacquemus’ new campaign takes on the “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7cfd336d-3af4-4f1c-8423-a5c6e63980b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Clichés</a><span>” about their products and marketing tactics.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d84d74fc-c706-4c1b-9187-016be953f9f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Emma Chamberlain</a><span> is selling 100 of her iconic pieces on eBay.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Tate McRae is all in on the bleached eyebrow look in the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3bd1d17f-71d8-4088-adda-065f240f0454?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Cruise 2026 Valentino campaign</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>E.l.f. challenges consumers to ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/52cff62a-0227-4544-ab63-700f494307d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Give an e.l.f.</a><span>’ in purpose-led campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c018a959-8bb7-446a-ae35-3b09baa9912a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Skinxiety</a><span>’: Noli </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/24084d98-317a-416d-890f-723bdfa2df08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launches</a><span> debut brand campaign, by INGO Stockholm.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ferragamo’s Hug Bag takes center stage in Matteo Mosterts’ “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/119c8c3c-26a8-4ec9-9da2-84bc05dd10e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Affair</a><span>” film.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>MAC Cosmetics to launch in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75c0de77-af2d-4d19-9880-85f409be119b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sephora’s US stores</a><span> for first time.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Skims taps </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8ff6a1f7-3d58-41a4-9e14-966cd366d947?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ami Colé founder as Executive VP Beauty</a><span> as Kim Kardashian gears up to grow beauty offering.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ralph Lauren Corp </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/95205bd9-f8de-442b-aa54-1c2145da5fc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Q2 sales, profit surge</a><span> on new customer acquisition.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The North Face, Highsnobiety and Zalando on how a</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bd3b43ed-3e03-4a5b-ad20-8c37413c69f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> TikTok joke</a><span> inspired a global campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Photographer Duane Michals lenses </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/12a8d531-1016-4cb2-9b75-c726ea632d7b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jacob Elordi</a><span> for Bottega Veneta’s new campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The new </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2100fffe-217b-4455-b55e-81612759fd5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hoka x Halfdays sneaker collab</a><span> is peak snow bunny style.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d6d1389a-653f-417d-82a7-bfeb30699ca4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">HOUSE OF ERRORS</a><span> unveils immaculate “VITESSE” collection.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/90e810fc-99ba-493b-93cc-6033624da0f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Brick of Will</a><span>’ – ACE campaign supports rural communities in Vietnam.</span></p></li>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🦀 Report Break: Deloitte study found </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/77e7524f-e7f6-4aa9-9bf6-98fe619fdfdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">monthly average digital entertainment spending</a><span> rose 24% to $78 from $63 between 2024–2025, as Australians added more subscriptions and platforms successfully pushed through price increases.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Households now hold an average of 3.7 subscriptions, up from 3.3 last year and 2.3 in 2021.</p>
</blockquote>
